# Redump media catalog for Open PS2 Loader folders.
# C = PlayStation 2 CD. D = PlayStation 2 DVD. P = PlayStation (POPS).
# T = one executable serial on both CD and DVD. Size chooses the folder.
# PlayStation 2 media comes from the Redump PS2 dat (2026-06-15, 11774 discs):
# bin/cue dumps are CD and iso dumps are DVD. Those track types match the media
# field on http://redump.org/discs/system/ps2/media/cd and
# http://redump.org/discs/system/ps2/media/dvd (SLUS-20210 is CD, SLUS-21720
# is DVD-5). PlayStation serials come from the Redump PSX dat (2026-06-15,
# 10914 discs), the set listed at http://redump.org/discs/system/psx/.
# One serial per line: AAAA_BBB.CC, or an 11-character on-disc product code
# with the dot in the same place (LSP06010.10). T is one executable serial that
# Redump lists as both CD and DVD. An image of a T serial at or under the
# 80-minute CD user-data limit (737,280,000 bytes) goes to CD. A larger image
# goes to DVD. CD track metadata also goes to CD.
